- **simulate**: Train the team's war-gaming muscle on PAST campaigns — pick a campaign that already ran, war-game it as if it hadn't launched yet (blind to the results), then reveal what actually happened and score the difference. Every miss becomes a trap-library entry; every accurate war-game becomes an exemplar. Use when the user says "run a simulation", "practice war-game", "train on past campaigns", "build our trap library", or when the team is new to war-gaming and the library is empty. ([yobolabs/plugins](https://github.com/yobolabs/plugins/tree/HEAD/campaign-wargame/skills/simulate) · ★1)
- **simulate**: Use to train growthify on the team's own history — "blind replay", "growthify simulate", "replay a past growth initiative", "replay that launch/pricing change", "train growthify on history". NOT for practicing marketing-campaign war-games on past campaign sends — that is campaign-wargame:simulate; this skill replays GROWTH initiatives (feature launches, pricing changes, mechanic rollouts) through the growthify generation loop. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/simulated-measurement-noise-modeling))
- **simulated-mediation-recovery-evaluation**: Use when you need to verify that a mediation-based cis driver ranking (e.g., WFTM/Sobel-based) actually recovers true underlying cis-trans mediations before trusting it on real SCNA/expression data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/simulated-mediation-recovery-evaluation))
- **simulated-metabolomics-data-generation**: Use when benchmarking or validating imputation algorithms on metabolomics data and ground-truth values are needed to compute RMSE/MLCI, or when a controlled MNAR+LOD/MAR missingness setting must be reproduced. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/simulated-metabolomics-data-generation))
- **simulated-metagenome-benchmarking**: Use when evaluating a metagenomic gene predictor on simulated sequencing reads with known reference annotations, especially to quantify accuracy gains from an unsupervised retraining step as sequencing error rate increases. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/simulated-metagenome-benchmarking))
- **simulated-metagenome-read-generation**: Use when a benchmark requires realistic paired-end shotgun reads from known reference genomes so pipeline outputs (e.g., operons) can be scored against curated per-genome truth annotations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/simulated-metagenome-read-generation))
- **simulated-metagenome-read-generation**: Use when benchmarking a metagenomic classifier (Kraken, Megablast, PhymmBL, NBC) or measuring reads-per-minute throughput and you need reads with known taxonomic origins, or when a standard-error (HiSeq/MiSeq-like) and a high-error (simBA-5, 2% mismatch) read set must be contrasted. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/simulated-metagenome-read-generation))
- **simulated-metagenomic-dataset-benchmarking**: Use when evaluating or reproducing quality-filtering stringency and chimera-retention of amplicon (16S/ITS) pipelines on simulated datasets with known ground truth, e.g. comparing UPARSE truncation filtering to MICCA expected-error (EE) filtering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/simulated-metagenomic-dataset-benchmarking))
- **simulated-metagenomic-read-generation**: Use when benchmarking or ablation-testing a taxonomic classifier (e.g. k-mer embedding size, architecture variants) because labeled real reads with known species-of-origin are unavailable; reads must be simulated from genomes with known taxonomy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/simulated-metagenomic-read-generation))
- **simulated-mixed-profile-generation**: Use when a cell-type deconvolution model needs supervised training data but no ground-truth spatial compositions exist, and a single-cell RNA-seq reference with cell type annotations is available. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/simulated-mixed-profile-generation))
- **simulated-multi-omics-dataset-generation**: Use when benchmarking integrative clustering methods on multi-dataset omic data and you need ground-truth labels across settings like similar datasets, noisy variables (sparse k-means variant), and datasets with different noise levels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/simulated-multi-omics-dataset-generation))
- **simulated-multidataset-clustering-generation**: Use when benchmarking KLIC/COCA-style integrative consensus clustering on synthetic data with known true labels — e.g. to quantify ARI gains of combining kernels versus single-dataset clustering and to inspect learned kernel weights. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/simulated-multidataset-clustering-generation))
- **simulated-mutation-catalog-generation**: Use when you need catalogs of known ground-truth signature composition (e.g., dilution series of SBS2 1–10% or SBS-MM1 1–20% into an average MM background) to benchmark signature fitting or bootstrap CI coverage. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/simulated-mutation-catalog-generation))
- **simulated-mzml-export**: Use when a ViMMS 2.0 Environment run (TopNController DDA or AIF DIA simulation over generated chemicals) has finished and the resulting scan list must be persisted as a well-formed mzML document for downstream evaluation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/simulated-mzml-export))
- **simulated-nmr-spectral-dataset-construction**: Use when you need a ground-truth-controlled benchmark to compare NMR spectra processing methods (binning, alignment like CluPA/icoshift, or peak-based approaches) where real spectra lack known per-peak labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/simulated-nmr-spectral-dataset-construction))
- **simulated-omics-data-generation**: Use when a scalability or performance benchmark of decoupleR's activity-inference methods is needed and a controlled synthetic omics matrix must be produced instead of real perturbation data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/simulated-omics-data-generation))
- **simulated-pacbio-read-generation**: Use when a de novo transcript reconstruction method must be evaluated against a known ground truth: simulate full-length PacBio Iso-Seq reads from multigene family isoforms at varying depths and abundances before running IsoCon or ICE. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/simulated-pacbio-read-generation))
- **simulated-patient-experience**: Author and compile conversational simulated patient instruction prompts for Carmen AI Assistant from faculty case documents, student handouts, activity instructions, and evaluation criteria. Supports both focused single-profession encounters and multi-discipline interprofessional (IPE) scenarios across separated or combined documents. ([jcyhsiao/oso-simulated-patient](https://github.com/jcyhsiao/oso-simulated-patient/tree/HEAD/authoring-skills/simulated-patient-experience))
- **simulated-randomers-training-data-augmentation**: Use when building training datasets for ONT dRNA basecalling/anomaly models from public or IVT data of questionable quality, and inference artefacts or 'hallucinations' from near-identical modified structures must be mitigated before training NanoListener/IsolationForest models. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/simulated-randomers-training-data-augmentation))
- **simulated-read-background-splicing**: Use when benchmarking a fusion-gene detection tool and you need test FASTQs containing known fusion junctions at controlled expression/coverage levels embedded in realistic sequencing background. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/simulated-read-background-splicing))
- **simulated-read-basecalling-accuracy-evaluation**: Use when you have simulated ONT nanopore signals (e.g. POD5 from NanoSimFormer's simulate with preset ont_r1041_dna_5khz) and need a quantitative readout of signal fidelity via basecalling accuracy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/simulated-read-basecalling-accuracy-evaluation))
- **simulated-read-benchmark-dataset-handling**: Use when comparing transcriptome assemblers on complex multi-isoform genes (e.g., GYPC, MAPT, BIN1) from simulated barcoded RNA-seq reads, and you need per-gene full-length isoform recovery counts to rank standard bulk assemblers vs barcode-aware methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/simulated-read-benchmark-dataset-handling))
- **simulated-read-benchmark-evaluation**: Use when a new algorithmic variant (e.g., a segmentation or alignment strategy) must be quantified as a speedup and quality change on simulated long-read data with a known reference genome. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/simulated-read-benchmark-evaluation))
- **simulated-read-benchmark-evaluation**: Use when you must quantify a reconstruction method's sensitivity and accuracy on known ground truth, e.g., verifying that rare variants down to 0.1% frequency at ~5000x coverage are recovered from simulated reads. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/simulated-read-benchmark-evaluation))
- **simulated-read-benchmarking**: Use when evaluating a streaming read classifier against large, repetitive pan-genome indexes and you need ground-truth labels; simulated Nanopore/PacBio reads give known target vs. nontarget origins for computing accuracy and F1. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/simulated-read-benchmarking))
- **simulated-read-benchmarking-across-simulators**: Use when evaluating whether simulated nanopore reads are fit for downstream analysis, specifically when reproducing the paper's de novo bacterial assembly benchmark across E. coli, K. pneumoniae, M. morganii, P. aeruginosa, and P. mirabilis genomes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/simulated-read-benchmarking-across-simulators))
- **simulated-read-classification-benchmarking**: Use when evaluating a read-level metagenomic classifier (e.g., Kaiju vs Kraken/Clark) against a ground truth: simulate reads with ART, classify them, and measure sensitivity and precision. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/simulated-read-classification-benchmarking))
- **simulated-read-dataset-generation**: Use when benchmarking long-read mapper runtime or accuracy and controlled simulated PacBio CLR datasets at multiple sequencing coverages (10x–40x) are needed as identical inputs for all tools. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/simulated-read-dataset-generation))
- **simulated-read-demultiplexing-benchmark**: Use when benchmarking or reimplementing barcode demultiplexing on simulated long-read FASTQ data, or when the analysis goal is to verify that a demultiplexer reproduces Flexiplex's unique--match assignment behaviour and reported accuracy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/simulated-read-demultiplexing-benchmark))
- **simulated-read-generation**: Use when building a classifier-evaluation benchmark that requires simulated Illumina metagenomic reads with known clade-of-origin labels per read, such as Kraken clade-exclusion experiments on simBA-5. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/simulated-read-generation))
- **simulated-read-generation-and-downsampling**: Use when benchmarking SV callers on controlled data: simulated SLR reads with known ground-truth SVs are needed, or long-read coverage must be systematically varied (5×–30×) to test coverage sensitivity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/simulated-read-generation-and-downsampling))
- **simulated-read-ground-truth-comparison**: Use when validating a metatranscriptome annotator's accuracy: you have simulated reads (e.g. MetaSim gut microbiome) with known ground-truth organism and SEED Subsystems labels and need to quantify genus-level and functional annotation accuracy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/simulated-read-ground-truth-comparison))
- **simulated-read-microdiversity-validation**: Use when a metagenomic profiling module's coverage-, breadth-, or nucleotide-diversity outputs must be validated against a reference genome with known simulated mapping properties before trusting real-sample results. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/simulated-read-microdiversity-validation))
- **simulated-read-truth-evaluation**: Use when comparing barcode demultiplexing tools on simulated single-cell long-read data where ground-truth read-barcode labels and a true barcode whitelist are available, and accuracy/FDR must be scored per edit-distance setting. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/simulated-read-truth-evaluation))
- **simulated-read-variant-calling-validation**: Use when simulated sequencing reads have been generated with an injected pathogenic variant and the dataset must be certified for educational variant-calling exercises before release to students. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/simulated-read-variant-calling-validation))
- **simulated-readset-benchmark-comparison**: Use when benchmarking plasmid assembler accuracy on simulated read sets: QUAST genome fraction, indels/mismatches per 100 kbp, missed-plasmid counts and fragmented-assembly counts vs a curated ground truth. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/simulated-readset-benchmark-comparison))
- **simulated-rnaseq-ground-truth-validation**: Use when simulated RNA-seq FASTQ reads (Polyester-generated with a known DE ground truth) must be checked that quantification, DESeq2 testing, and GO enrichment reproduce the injected signal before release as a teaching dataset. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/simulated-rnaseq-ground-truth-validation))
- **simulated-rnaseq-labelled-data-preparation**: Use when a supervised combination of DEG detection methods is needed and no labelled RNA-seq data exist, requiring simulated spike-in/training data with known DEG truth labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/simulated-rnaseq-labelled-data-preparation))
- **simulated-rnaseq-tool-benchmarking**: Use when comparing IPA-detection tools on RNA-seq data with no matched 3'-end-seq or Iso-seq ground truth, or when assessing how sequencing depth (10X–50X) affects identification accuracy and PAU quantification error. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/simulated-rnaseq-tool-benchmarking))
- **simulated-rnaseq-truth-label-comparison**: Use when you need to evaluate or tune how multiple DEG detection methods (DESeq2, NOISeq, limma) are combined, by comparing vote-threshold or combined-FDR calls against simulated RNA-seq data with known truth labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/simulated-rnaseq-truth-label-comparison))
- **simulated-scrna-seq-benchmarking**: Use when comparing cell-embedding methods (e.g., scGAE vs scDeepCluster, DCA, scVI, Ivis, SAUCIE, scGNN, ZINB-WaVE, PCA, PHATE) on simulated scRNA-seq data where ground-truth cluster labels are available and clustering quality must be quantified by NMI. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/simulated-scrna-seq-benchmarking))
- **simulated-scrna-seq-clustering-benchmark**: Use when a simulated single-cell gene expression matrix must be clustered without a pre-specified cluster count, and cluster-merging behavior must be traced against the d_bar/2 adaptive stopping criterion. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/simulated-scrna-seq-clustering-benchmark))
- **simulated-scrna-seq-count-matrix-generation**: Use when benchmarking cell-type annotation tools requires scRNA-seq datasets with known ground-truth labels (e.g. simulated PBMC count matrices at varying cell counts) so per-cell annotation accuracy can be computed without manual curation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/simulated-scrna-seq-count-matrix-generation))
- **simulated-scrna-seq-quality-evaluation**: Use when you have simulated scRNA-seq cells (e.g., simulated.h5ad from GRouNdGAN --generate) and must verify they are realistic and non-collapsed — especially on heterogeneous multi-cell-type references like PBMC-All. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/simulated-scrna-seq-quality-evaluation))
- **simulated-scrnaseq-benchmark-data-handling**: Use when comparing runtime and memory efficiency of scRNA-Seq TE quantification tools (e.g., SoloTE vs scTE) on the same simulated BAM inputs across genome versions such as hg38 and mm10. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/simulated-scrnaseq-benchmark-data-handling))
- **simulated-scrnaseq-benchmark-reproduction**: Use when a simulated scRNA-seq AnnData with high-resolution Leiden labels must be used to benchmark enrichment-scoring methods on per-cluster binary classification, or to verify that negative-gene-set-subtraction variants outperform standard scoring. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/simulated-scrnaseq-benchmark-reproduction))
- **simulated-scrnaseq-cell-type-benchmarking**: Use when a simulated or pre-labeled scRNA-seq count matrix plus cell-type marker list are available and the goal is to quantitatively rank multiple cell-type identification methods by accuracy and F1-score. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/simulated-scrnaseq-cell-type-benchmarking))
- **simulated-scrnaseq-clustering-benchmarking**: Use when you need to quantify whether a cell-cell similarity-driven embedding (cMAP) improves clustering of simulated scRNA-seq expression matrices over PCA or Leiden baselines, using adjusted Rand index against ground-truth cell labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/simulated-scrnaseq-clustering-benchmarking))
- **simulated-scrnaseq-count-data-generation**: Use when you need to benchmark gene/feature-selection methods on synthetic scRNA-seq count data with known informative genes and controlled subpopulation rarity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/simulated-scrnaseq-count-data-generation))
- **simulated-scrnaseq-count-generation**: Use when you need a controlled scRNA-seq simulation fixture (known clusters, target/control gene partition, true labels) to evaluate confounding-effect removal or control-gene-set robustness for scPLS-type methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/simulated-scrnaseq-count-generation))
- **simulated-scrnaseq-data-generation**: Use when benchmarking source-to-target transfer clustering (e.g., NMF dictionary transfer + SC3) requires synthetic count matrices with exact ground-truth labels, or when comparing complete / incomplete / no-overlap source-target cell-type overlap settings. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/simulated-scrnaseq-data-generation))
- **simulated-scrnaseq-dataset-processing**: Use when evaluating or validating cellular barcode matching accuracy for long-read/short-read single-cell RNA-seq tools on simulated LR-SR datasets with known composition, e.g. reproducing a Table 3-style accuracy comparison. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/simulated-scrnaseq-dataset-processing))
- **simulated-scrnaseq-ground-truth-matching**: Use when simulated single-cell expression matrices (with known TE-cell labels and ground-truth log2(FC)) must be scored against a tool's recovered markers, e.g. benchmarking SoloTE vs scTE in Fig 3. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/simulated-scrnaseq-ground-truth-matching))
- **simulated-scrnaseq-ground-truth-validation**: Use when evaluating or comparing clustering methods on scRNA-seq data where simulated ground-truth cluster labels exist, especially to test whether a method resolves rare subpopulations placed close together. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/simulated-scrnaseq-ground-truth-validation))
- **simulated-scrnaseq-trajectory-generation**: Use when benchmarking trajectory inference or RNA-velocity-based methods against known ground truth, or when replicated simulated datasets (e.g., 785 cells x 600 genes cycle-tree) are needed to quantify pseudotime accuracy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/simulated-scrnaseq-trajectory-generation))
- **simulated-scseq-cluster-count-evaluation**: Use when you need to benchmark single-cell clustering methods on data with known ground-truth cell-type labels, especially to test whether a method recovers the correct number of clusters without a predetermined K. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/simulated-scseq-cluster-count-evaluation))
- **simulated-sequence-dataset-benchmarking**: Use when comparing segmentation behavior of MEM-based aligners (FMAlign2 vs FMAlign) across controlled similarity gradients and MEM coverage values, or when sweeping the minimum MEM length (-l) on ultra-long nucleotide FASTA inputs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/simulated-sequence-dataset-benchmarking))
- **simulated-single-cell-expression-data-generation**: Use when you need controlled-size dense scRNA-seq test data (e.g., 1,000 to 250,000 cells, 10,000 genes) to benchmark query runtimes of AnnSQL, AnnData (backed or in-memory), or Seurat without depending on real atlas files. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/simulated-single-cell-expression-data-generation))
- **simulated-spatial-dataset-generation**: Use when benchmarking the scalability or runtime of a spatial colorization method (e.g., Spaco) and you need controlled synthetic SRT inputs with known cell counts and labels instead of real tissue sections. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/simulated-spatial-dataset-generation))
- **simulated-spectra-ground-truth-comparison**: Use when evaluating quantification accuracy of NMR spectral deconvolution methods on simulated spectra where ground-truth metabolite concentrations are known and methods can be regressed against truth. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/simulated-spectra-ground-truth-comparison))
- **simulated-st-benchmark-dataset-handling**: Use when benchmarking cell type deconvolution methods on simulated ST data where ground-truth per-spot cell type proportions are known, or when reproducing SPACEL/Spoint benchmark metrics (PCC/SSIM/RMSE/JSD/AS) on the 32 pseudo-spot datasets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/simulated-st-benchmark-dataset-handling))
- **simulated-st-data-validation**: Use when a deconvolution method for multi-cellular pixel-resolution ST data must be quantitatively benchmarked against ground-truth cell-type proportions, especially when a matched scRNA-seq/MERFISH reference is or is not available. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/simulated-st-data-validation))
- **simulated-test-data-evaluation**: Use when benchmarking deletion-target classifiers or parameter predictors on simulated expression test sets with known 'Redundant'/'Unique' labels and true OU parameters, or when measuring robustness to added noise. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/simulated-test-data-evaluation))
- **simulated-time-course-metabolomics-data-handling**: Use when benchmarking inference of absolute concentrations from relative abundance time-course GC-MS data and ground-truth response factors are needed that cannot be measured experimentally. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/simulated-time-course-metabolomics-data-handling))
- **simulated-toy-dataset-generation**: Use when validating or benchmarking a clustering algorithm (e.g. KMD linkage) against known structure: you need reproducible synthetic datasets with controllable cluster shapes before testing on real biological data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/simulated-toy-dataset-generation))

- **simulation-based-benchmark-evaluation**: Use when comparing insertion/NRS-calling tools for depth robustness: a simulated linked-read dataset with ground truth exists and the goal is to quantify how recall and precision degrade as coverage drops from 65x toward 13x. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/simulation-based-benchmark-evaluation))
- **simulation-based-benchmark-evaluation**: Use when a variant-calling or clustering method must be validated against simulated data with a known ground-truth mutation list, especially to test scalability to larger sample sizes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/simulation-based-benchmark-evaluation))
- **simulation-based-benchmark-evaluation-scrnaseq**: Use when benchmarking an scRNA-Seq imputation/denoising method (e.g. AutoClass vs DCA) against a known ground truth, or when testing robustness to hyperparameters such as bottleneck size, pre-clustering number K, or classifier weight. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/simulation-based-benchmark-evaluation-scrnaseq))
- **simulation-based-calibration**: Stage 4 of the Bayesian workflow and its honesty core: simulation-based calibration checking (SBC) — check whether the model's stated uncertainties are actually correct, not just whether it fits. Covers why one recovery run can't validate inference, the SBC loop and its uniform-rank theorem, test quantities T(theta, y), near-independence and tie-breaking, the two purposes (algorithm coherence and generator-vs-posterior code equivalence), reading rank histograms and the ECDF-difference plot with the gamma uniformity metric, keeping SBC honest under wide priors (tighter priors, rejection sampli… ([3shn/baywright](https://github.com/3shn/baywright/tree/HEAD/skills/simulation-based-calibration))
- **simulation-based-cna-caller-benchmarking**: Use when you need to compare precision and sensitivity of single-cell CNA callers against simulated ground-truth events (200 kb–5 Mb single-copy losses/gains) on high-coverage scWGS benchmark data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/simulation-based-cna-caller-benchmarking))
- **simulation-based-count-data-analysis**: Use when validating a differential expression method on scRNA-seq count data with known ground truth — e.g. testing whether a covariate such as t2d_sim is associated with expression while controlling type-I error and estimating power. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/simulation-based-count-data-analysis))
- **simulation-based-de-power-evaluation**: Use when evaluating or comparing DE method power on single-cell RNA-seq data where simulated ground truth (known DE genes, coverage rate, tau1/tau0 parameters) is available, or when reproducing iDEA power-gain results. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/simulation-based-de-power-evaluation))
- **simulation-based-estimation-error-evaluation**: Use when evaluating whether a scalable algorithmic variant (e.g. chunked E-step with Chunk=TRUE, chunk.size=500 or 1000) reproduces the accuracy of a reference implementation while reducing runtime, or when benchmarking confounding-factor inference on simulated scRNAseq grids. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/simulation-based-estimation-error-evaluation))
- **simulation-based-gene-recovery-validation**: Use when you need to verify that a Bayesian-FDR intrinsic/differential gene selection procedure recovers known true DE genes in simulation and yields biologically interpretable enriched pathways on real data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/simulation-based-gene-recovery-validation))
- **simulation-based-grn-method-benchmarking**: Use when evaluating or comparing cell-specific network inference methods (e.g. LIONESS with Pearson, SSN, pySCENIC) and synthetic ground truth is required because real single-cell data lacks a known regulatory network reference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/simulation-based-grn-method-benchmarking))
- **simulation-based-inference**: When and how to use simulation-based inference (SBI / likelihood-free inference) instead of stochastic sampling, and how to validate it before any scientific use. Use for inference problems with an intractable or expensive likelihood, amortized inference over many events, or when classical sampling is too slow — and always before trusting an SBI posterior. ([Uddiptaatwork/praxis](https://github.com/Uddiptaatwork/praxis/tree/HEAD/skills/simulation-based-inference) · ★2)

- **simulation-based-method-benchmarking**: Use when a paper claims a deconvolution or inference method outperforms a competitor and you must reproduce the comparison on simulated mixtures with known ground-truth subpopulation profiles, such as 40x GCB-monocyte bisulfite data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/simulation-based-method-benchmarking))
- **simulation-based-method-benchmarking**: Use when comparing cell-type deconvolution or clustering methods on spatial transcriptomics data where ground truth is unavailable in real tissue, by simulating multi-cellular pixels with known cell-type proportions and evaluating RMSE of estimated proportions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/simulation-based-method-benchmarking))
- **simulation-based-method-comparison**: Use when you need to rank or validate differential abundance testing methods against ground truth on synthetic compositional count data, e.g. at low replicate numbers where FDR control is critical. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/simulation-based-method-comparison))
- **simulation-based-method-evaluation**: Use when evaluating a CNA caller's sensitivity/precision across variant sizes and coverages, or choosing bin size and depth for scWGS analysis using simulated data with known truth events. - **simulation-based-method-validation-against-ground-truth**: Use when a new distance-estimation or differential-state method must be checked for accuracy and calibration: simulated data with known per-cell-type true distances are available or can be generated (here via scDist::simData with nct=10, N1=N2=5). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/simulation-based-method-validation-against-ground-truth))
- **simulation-based-model-benchmarking**: Use when you need to validate that a kinetic rate-inference pipeline recovers known parameter values, by simulating data (e.g. 1000 genes, 2 replicates, 20 min labelling pulse) and correlating inferred vs expected outputs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/simulation-based-model-benchmarking))
- **simulation-based-power-and-type-i-error-estimation**: Use when comparing statistical meta-analysis or testing methods' empirical power and type I error across scenarios, using simulated summary statistics rather than real data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/simulation-based-power-and-type-i-error-estimation))
- **simulation-based-scrna-seq-data-generation**: Use when a null or power study needs synthetic single-cell count data with excess zeros, overdispersion, and within-sample correlation, e.g. to benchmark ZI-model specifications or type-I error of regression-based DE methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/simulation-based-scrna-seq-data-generation))
- **simulation-based-single-cell-power-analysis**: Use when you need to validate an analytic power-analysis method for multi-sample scRNA-seq DE studies against ground-truth simulations, or when you must quantify the runtime/memory cost of simulation-based power tools. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/simulation-based-single-cell-power-analysis))
- **simulation-based-type-i-error-and-power-evaluation**: Use when assessing whether a gene-level DE test (likelihood ratio, mean-model Z, or Stouffer Z_combined) preserves nominal type-I error and yields interpretable power on simulated scRNA-seq counts with within-sample correlation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/simulation-based-type-i-error-and-power-evaluation))
- **simulation-based-type-i-error-estimation**: Use when validating a new or competing multivariate association test on simulated count data: you need empirical type I error under β = 0 and empirical power under alternatives varying signal density (10%, 20%, 30%) and effect size. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/simulation-based-type-i-error-estimation))
- **simulation-based-validation**: Use when when you need to verify that a statistical correction (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v1/skills/simulation-based-validation))
- **simulation-based-variant-caller-benchmarking**: Use when comparing SNV calling accuracy between short-read-based (e.g. GATK, FreeBayes) and SMS read-based (e.g. Longshot) callers, and no unbiased truth set exists — simulation provides ground-truth variants for precision/recall quantification. - **simulation-benchmark-comparison**: Use when evaluating a circRNA full-length assembly/quantification tool against simulated paired-end FASTQ reads with known ground-truth circular isoforms, to characterize how sequencing depth or read length affects assembly sensitivity, precision, and F1. - **simulation-benchmark-figure-generation**: Use when validating a kinetic-rate inference pipeline on synthetic data: you need a simulated RNA-life-cycle benchmark with known ground-truth synthesis/processing/export/degradation rates and corresponding benchmark figures. - **simulation-benchmark-method-comparison**: Use when you have simulated or sub-sampled high-dimensional metabolomics p-values plus competing correlation matrices (e.g., Pearson vs distance correlation) and need to benchmark eigen-analysis-based Meff estimation methods against a gold standard by RMSE. - **simulation-benchmark-stratification**: Use when evaluating assembly/quantification accuracy on simulated data where performance may depend on structural complexity (e.g., overlapping genomic regions among circRNAs), requiring metrics stratified by a complexity bin such as RI 1-4 vs RI &gt; 4.
